- **change-point-detector**: Bayesian Online Change-Point Detection (BOCPD) on a ticker's daily log returns. Detects points in time where the return-generating distribution changed (regime shift in mean, vol, or both), reports the confidence at each detected boundary, and emits per-segment statistics (annualized return, annualized vol) so the reader can see what changed.
